You feel the weight of it, and your brain immediately wants to know what happened next.\\n\\nThe weak version of that same story sounds something like, \"For a while I\\'d been unhappy at work, my coworker said something, my manager didn\\'t back me up, and eventually I just decided to leave.\" Same experience, completely different pull. One makes you lean in, one gives you a reason to keep scrolling past.\\n\\nThere\\'s a concept called the curiosity gap, which is the distance between what your audience already knows and what they want to know. A strong hook opens that gap honestly, and a good story closes it. That\\'s the whole thing.\\n\\nIf your content isn\\'t getting the traction you want, go back and read your opening line out loud.
